Across a wide range of organisms (E. coli, pig heart, Lactobacillus, yeast, etc.), the predominant quaternary structure is a **homodimer**, where two identical subunits interact via their NAD⁺-binding domains.<br><br>**Step 3: Evaluate each option.**<br>- **A.
